- **fdd-validate**: FDD 的验证流水线——里程碑 / 最终批量闸。一条线性、成本递增的三级 gate：静态验证（scrutiny-validator 硬门禁 test/lint/type-check + 逐 feature scrutiny）→ 代码审查（code-reviewer，逐 feature）→ user-test（运行时断言探测）。由 harness-stack:fdd-execution 在里程碑收口（scope=milestone：加条件 security-auditor + 治理反馈 + seal）与循环跑空（scope=final：加 coverage gate + fdd gate）时调用。 ([wanggang316/harness-stack](https://github.com/wanggang316/harness-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/deprecated/fdd-validate) · ★2)
- **fdd-validation-contract**: 为一个 plan 撰写 validation contract——把 definition of done 落成一组可测试、用户可观测的 assertion（VAL-&lt;AREA&gt;-NNN），带 persona 与声明的 Evidence。它是 fdd step 1（规划）里的 contract 阶段。契约通过逐 area 的 investigation subagent 与若干轮 adversarial review 构建，而非一人独写。产出 .harness-runtime/plans/&lt;slug&gt;/validation-contract.md，并经由 fdd init-state 播种 validation-state.json。在项目内首次使用时，还会 bootstrap 项目级约定文档 docs/user-test-patterns.md。 ([wanggang316/harness-stack](https://github.com/wanggang316/harness-stack/tree/HEAD/skills/deprecated/fdd-validation-contract) · ★2)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--cd59c884295c7b6c))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many gene sets (e.g., GO/KEGG terms) have been tested per ICA metagene Sk vector via hypergeometric overrepresentation or GSEA and p-values must be corrected before calling enriched sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--bc6d9004ccce6119))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many features are tested independently (one test per metabolomic feature) and raw p-values must be FDR-adjusted — e.g. reproducing Fig. 3f-style significant-feature counts at FDR &lt;= 0.2 on batch-corrected ST000868 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--2e428926da972ecd))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a between-group abundance test on LC-MS feature tables (e.g. 94 samples, 14 lamprey tissues, negative ion mode) yields raw per-feature P values across thousands of mass features, and a significance cutoff must control the false-discovery rate before filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--8eb5a7b891cb22ad))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when testing hundreds to thousands of omics features (metabolomics peaks, RNA-seq genes) for group differences in BiomiX-style pipelines and raw p-values must be FDR-corrected before filtering at p.adj thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--50c97eb2dfbc5833))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Decision support for identifying differentially abundant or expressed gene clusters across sample groups when thousands of clusters are tested simultaneously and raw p-values would otherwise inflate false positives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--f0a5955ab17e6099))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a lipidomic dataset is compared across two or more experimental groups with a test per lipid feature (t-test/ANOVA), and corrected p-values plus significance asterisks are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--c35239da10e9cdb7))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when thousands of pathway-level importance scores (VIP, MB-VIP, RGCCA loadings, or sparse-DIABLO bootstrap non-zero-loading statistics) must be tested against permutation nulls and converted into pathway detection calls with controlled false discovery rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--128c4c3faeeb4e1d))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a metabolomics or multi-omics analysis yields thousands of pairwise correlation P-values (e.g., metabolite-protein Spearman correlations across ~202 shared samples) and edges must be called at FDR &lt; 0.05 with an effect-size cutoff such as |r| &gt; 0.3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--9d08e46bf2c6165c))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when testing differential relative abundances of many taxa (phylum to genus level, e.g. l2/l5/l6 tables) or pooling taxa estimates across studies, and raw p-values must be FDR-adjusted before calling significant taxa. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when testing thousands of features (e.g., 28,417 genes across 2491 tissue spots) for spatial variability or differential signal and multiplicity-controlled significance calls (BY-adjusted FDR ≤ 0.05) are needed for feature selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a per-gene test across many genes (e.g. 100-gene Lamian XDE/TDE permutation likelihood-ratio tests) yields raw p-values that must be corrected for multiplicity before calling genes at FDR.overall &lt; 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--f0cc09d153136de3))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many statistical tests (e.g., Fisher's exact tests of resource-annotation enrichment across SignaLink, NetPath, CancerSEA terms) are run simultaneously and per-test p-values must be corrected before calling over-/underrepresentation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--afc015f10fd37ba1))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a DE analysis (limma on RNA-seq counts, e.g., ExpressAnalystR PerformDEAnal output) yields thousands of raw p-values and DEGs must be called at a defensible multiple-testing-adjusted threshold matching a reference publication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--dd013379d715aa90))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a per-feature hypothesis test (e.g., Wald test on Tweedie/CPLM/ZICP model coefficients) produces many p-values that must be FDR-adjusted to make differential-expression calls across genes or taxa. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--81dac562bf3faac5))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when performing thousands of per-gene group comparisons (e.g. t-tests across all probes in a blood expression matrix) where module-level hits would otherwise be inflated, and a BH-FDR-adjusted p-value cutoff must be applied instead of raw p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--44a63ab025ad1847))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when performing large-scale single-cell hypothesis testing (co-expression edges, DE, CRISPR screen P values, or pathway enrichment over network edges) where thousands to millions of simultaneous tests require explicit FDR or Bonferroni correction before calling significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--844e52ba03707869))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when calling significant sQTLs (or other high-multiple-testing association scans) from a nominal association pass over GTEx-style transcript-expression and genotype files, where per-test P-values must be converted to empirical, permutation-calibrated FDR values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--729be9fa8bc06d79))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when many TF perturbation simulations each yield a p-value and you must correct across all tested targets before calling significant hits (e.g., FDR &lt; 0.01 plus log2FC &gt; 0.5 in a 200+ TF screen). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--d81efc8744afcf82))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when calling significant differential state (DS) or differential detection genes across many cell clusters/genes in multi-sample multi-condition scRNA-seq data, where per-gene p-values must be adjusted to control FDR before hit calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction--a32ae25234ec1f2e))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-correction-pathway-enrichment**: Use when per-pathway raw p-values (e.g., from eSPIA impact analysis or gene/metabolite ORA) must be corrected across many pathways, or when p-values from joint gene-metabolite tests need combination before calling significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-correction-pathway-enrichment))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-first-step-screening**: Use when regression covariates number in the thousands to ~hundred thousand (TCGA mRNA entries or mutated genes), the multiple-testing problem is of concern, and variables must be screened before penalized regression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-first-step-screening))
- **fdr-multiple-testing-thresholding**: Use when a spatial transcriptomics DE method (C-SIDE, SPARK-X) returns per-gene p-values across many genes and cell types, and significant gene calls must be tabulated at a controlled FDR level such as 1%. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-multiple-testing-thresholding))
- **fdr-power-evaluation-simulation**: Use when validating that a conditional independence testing method controls FDR at target levels and comparing its power (TPR) against linear/Gaussian baselines like OLS on semi-synthetic single-cell data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/fdr-power-evaluation-simulation))
- **fdr-qvalue-rank-assignment**: Use when a feature table already carries FDR-adjusted p-values (q-values) from feature-wise tests plus multivariate importance ranks (e.g., MUVR RF 'mid' model), and the goal is to pick the most robust metabolic features for downstream identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-qvalue-rank-assignment))
- **fdr-significance-annotation**: Use when ROIs from disease-vs-control NMR spectra (e.g. serum from lung cancer vs controls) have been scored for trend ('+'/'-') and significance ('*'/'!') and metabolites must be classified into the six ROIAL-NMR categories in the final dataResult table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-significance-annotation))
- **fdr-significance-threshold-filtering**: Use when a differential expression or enrichment result table contains per-feature FDR-adjusted p-values and you must classify features as significant using a study-defined threshold (e.g., FDR &lt; 0.1 for the salamander ortholog-level results). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-significance-threshold-filtering))
- **fdr-target-decoy-cutoff-validation**: Use when a scored target-decoy TG annotation list (e.g. ResultsTG from ScoreLipids) must be filtered to an empirical FDR (default 0.1) and validated against a standards file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-target-decoy-cutoff-validation))
- **fdr-threshold-based-isoform-selection**: Use when per-isoform significance calls are available from multiple differential expression runs or multiple statistical methods, and a robust consensus DIU isoform set is needed before acorde co-usage clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fdr-threshold-based-isoform-selection))
- **fdr-threshold-based-reproducible-feature-selection**: Use when you have MS-Metabolomics replicate data (technical or biological replicates, e.g. COPDGene Tech/BioTech/Bio) and need to flag which metabolites and sample pairs are reproducible while controlling FDR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr-threshold-based-reproducible-feature-selection))
- **fdr-threshold-gene-intersection**: Use when comparing DS/DE method outputs across replicates to assess concordance of significant gene calls (p_adj &lt; 0.05) and classify shared versus method-unique hits as true or false discoveries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-threshold-gene-intersection))
- **fdr-threshold-sweep-analysis**: Use when comparing imputation methods (Bfimpute, scImpute, SAVER, VIPER, DrImpute, MAGIC) on a paired scRNA-seq/bulk dataset by measuring how imputation changes agreement between single-cell and bulk DE gene sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-threshold-sweep-analysis))
- **fdr-thresholded-credible-effects-interpretation**: Use when scRNA-seq cell-type count tables per sample (e.g. from Scanpy) have been modeled with scCODA and you must decide which cell-type abundance changes survive FDR control, e.g. FDR 0.2 in the Salmonella/H. polygyrus SCP44 dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-thresholded-credible-effects-interpretation))
- **fdr-thresholded-differential-expression-calling**: Use when you have per-gene FDR result tables from RNA-seq mixed model fits (e.g. kimma lme/lmerel models) and need to define DEG sets at FDR &lt; 0.05, or compare DEG detection between two model formulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-thresholded-differential-expression-calling))
- **fdr-thresholded-droplet-calling**: Use when you need a model-based baseline droplet filter for raw 10X (CellRanger raw_feature_bc_matrix) snRNA-seq or scRNA-seq count matrices, or when comparing EmptyDrops calls to DIEM debris-score or quantile filtering on the same droplets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-thresholded-droplet-calling))
- **fdr-thresholded-loop-count-reproducibility-checking**: Use when a ChIA-PET loop-calling result (MICC FDR-filtered interactions) must be reproduced against a published interaction count and validated by anchor ChIP-seq co-occupancy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fdr-thresholded-loop-count-reproducibility-checking))
- **fdr-thresholded-power-estimation**: Use when simulations provide a known set of true DE genes and you must quantify detection power at a controlled FDR level (e.g., true FDR 5%) and compare methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-thresholded-power-estimation))
- **fdr-thresholded-significance-calling**: Use when Normalisr association outputs (gRNA-gene TSS-repression, trans-association, differential expression, or co-expression P values) must be filtered into significant hits using stated Q-value and logFC thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fdr-thresholded-significance-calling))
- **fdr-tpr-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a conditional independence or feature-selection method claims FDR control and you need to quantify its empirical FDR and TPR against a ground truth (e.g. t-tests of perturbed vs unperturbed cells) and compare to OLS and marginal-test baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/fdr-tpr-benchmark-evaluation))
- **fdr-tpr-operating-point-analysis**: Use when evaluating a variable selection procedure that promises false discovery rate control: you need empirical FDR vs. TPR operating points across target FDR levels (e.g., 0.05, 0.1, 0.2) and across importance score functions of differing predictive adequacy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/fdr-tpr-operating-point-analysis))
- **fdr2d-isoline-visualization**: Use when a metabolomics feature-level results table (mean difference, standard error, p-value, fdr2d per hypothesis) must be visualized as the paper's Figure 8 volcano/tornado pair with fdr2d isolines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr2d-isoline-visualization))
- **fdr2d-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a metabolomics study (e.g. the 3226 x 57 schisandra Korea-vs-China matrix) requires simultaneous testing of thousands of metabolite null hypotheses under two-dimensional FDR control at levels such as 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, or 0.3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fdr2d-multiple-testing-correction))
